\displaystyle\sum_{i}E\left[v_{il}^{4}\left(G_{L}v\right)_{il}^{4}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}E\left[v_{il}^{4}\right]E\left[\left(G_{L}v\right)_{il}^{4}\right]\preceq\sum_{i}E\left[\left(G_{L}v\right)_{il}^{4}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j}G_{L,ij}^{4}E\left[v_{il}^{4}\right]+3\sum_{i}\sum_{j}\sum_{k\neq j}G_{L,ij}^{2}G_{L,ik}^{2}E\left[v_{il}^{2}\right]E\left[v_{jl}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\preceq\sum_{i}\sum_{j}\sum_{k}G_{L,ij}^{2}G_{L,ik}^{2}=\sum_{i}\left(G_{L}G_{L}^{\prime}\right)_{ii}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i}\sum_{j}\left(G_{L}G_{L}^{\prime}\right)_{ij}^{2}=||G_{L}G_{L}^{\prime}||_{F}^{2}.

The argument for
G_{U}
is analogous.
Now, I turn to showing convergence of the conditional variance.
With abuse of notation, let
W_{i}=s_{i}^{\prime}v_{i}
and
X_{i}=v_{i}^{\prime}A\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}+v_{i}^{\prime}A\left(G_{U}^{\prime}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}
.
Since
\textup{Var}\left(BT\right)=B^{2}\sum_{i}E\left[W_{i}^{2}\right]+B^{2}\sum_{i}E\left[X_{i}^{2}\right]=1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i}E\left[B^{2}y_{i}^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]-1

\displaystyle=B^{2}\sum_{i}\left(E\left[X_{i}^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]-E\left[X_{i}^{2}\right]\right)+2B^{2}\sum_{i}E\left[W_{i}X_{i}\mid\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt+B^{2}\sum_{i}\left(E\left[W_{i}^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]-E\left[W_{i}^{2}\right]\right).

The previous observations in the filtration do not feature, so
E\left[W_{i}^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]-E\left[W_{i}^{2}\right]=0
.
It suffices to show that the RHS converges to 0.
For the
\sum_{i}E\left[W_{i}X_{i}\mid\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]
term,

\displaystyle B^{2}\sum_{i}E\left[W_{i}X_{i}\mid\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=B^{2}\sum_{i}E\left[W_{i}\left(v_{i}^{\prime}A\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}+v_{i}^{\prime}A\left(G_{U}^{\prime}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\right)\mid\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=B^{2}\sum_{i}E\left[W_{i}v_{i}^{\prime}A\right]\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}+B^{2}\sum_{i}E\left[W_{i}v_{i}^{\prime}A\right]\left(G_{U}^{\prime}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}.

It suffices to show that the respective squares converge to 0.
Due to bounded fourth moments, and applying the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality repeatedly, for some n-vector
\delta_{v}
with
||\delta_{v}||_{2}\leq C
,

E\left[\left(\sum_{i}E[W_{i}v_{i}^{\prime}]A\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\right)^{2}\right]\preceq\delta_{v}^{\prime}G_{L}G_{L}^{\prime}\delta_{v}\leq||\delta_{v}||_{2}^{2}||G_{L}G_{L}^{\prime}||_{2}\preceq||G_{L}G_{L}^{\prime}||_{F},

and the same argument can be applied to the
G_{U}
term.
Finally,

\displaystyle\sum_{i}\left(E\left[X_{i}^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]-E\left[X_{i}^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\bigg(E\left[\left(v_{i}^{\prime}A\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}+v_{i}^{\prime}A\left(G_{U}^{\prime}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt-E\left[\left(v_{i}^{\prime}A\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}+v_{i}^{\prime}A\left(G_{U}^{\prime}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\right)^{2}\right]\bigg).

It suffices to consider the
G_{L}
term, as the
G_{U}
and cross terms are analogous:

\displaystyle\sum_{i}

\displaystyle\left(E\left[\left(v_{i}^{\prime}A\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]-E\left[\left(v_{i}^{\prime}A\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\right)^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\left(\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}A^{\prime}E\left[v_{i}v_{i}^{\prime}\right]A\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}-E\left[\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}A^{\prime}v_{i}v_{i}^{\prime}A\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\right]\right).

Since
\sum_{i}\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}A^{\prime}E\left[v_{i}v_{i}^{\prime}\right]A\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}
is demeaned, it suffices to show that its variance converges to 0.
Due to bounded moments,

\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i}\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}A^{\prime}E\left[v_{i}v_{i}^{\prime}\right]A\left(G_{L}v\right)_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\right)\preceq\sum_{i}\sum_{j}\left(G_{L}G_{L}^{\prime}\right)^{2}=||G_{L}G_{L}^{\prime}||_{F}^{2},

which suffices for the result.
∎

The full and latest version of the paper, including proofs of results in the appendices, can be found at
https://lutheryap.github.io/files/mwiv_het_wp.pdf
.

Appendix E
Supplementary Appendix, Not for Publication

E.1
Proofs for Appendix C

Proof of
Lemma

3
.

I begin with part (c). By applying the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i}C_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}h_{2}^{A}\left(i,j\right)R_{mj}\right)\left(\sum_{j\neq i}h_{2}^{B}\left(i,j\right)R_{mj}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{i}C_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}h_{2}^{A}\left(i,j\right)R_{mj}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{i}C_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}h_{2}^{B}\left(i,j\right)R_{mj}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i}C_{i}\left(\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}h_{2}^{A}\left(i,j\right)R_{mj}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}h_{2}^{B}\left(i,j\right)R_{mj}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i}C_{i}\left(\sum_{i}\tilde{R}_{mi}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{i}\tilde{R}_{mi}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\leq C\sum_{i}\tilde{R}_{mi}^{2}.

The proof of all other parts are entirely analogous.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

4
.

Proof of
Lemma

4
(a)
.

Using the decomposition from AS23,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}F_{ij}V_{1i}V_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ij}^{2}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)+\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}G_{ji}F_{ji}Cov\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j},V_{1j}V_{2j}V_{3i}V_{4i}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}G_{kj}F_{kj}Cov\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j},V_{1k}V_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}G_{jk}F_{jk}Cov\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j},V_{1j}V_{2j}V_{3k}V_{4k}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}G_{ik}F_{ik}Cov\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j},V_{1i}V_{2i}V_{3k}V_{4k}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}G_{ki}F_{ki}Cov\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j},V_{1k}V_{2k}V_{3i}V_{4i}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left[\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\right]\sum_{i}\left(\left(\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}F_{ij}\right)^{2}+\left(\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}F_{ij}\right)\left(\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ji}F_{ji}\right)\right).

Notice that the terms in
\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}
are absorbed into the sum over
k
so that the final expression can be written as
\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}
.
Then, due to
Assumption

3
(a) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}F_{ij}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{j\neq i}F_{ij}^{2}\right)\leq C\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}^{2},

and

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}F_{ij}\right)\left(\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ji}F_{ji}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}F_{ij}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ji}F_{ji}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ji}^{2}\right)^{1/2}=C\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}^{2}.

Proof of
Lemma

4
(b).

Expand the term:

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}V_{1i}V_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4j}=\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\left(R_{1i}R_{2k}+v_{1i}R_{2k}+R_{1i}v_{2k}+v_{1i}v_{2k}\right)V_{3j}V_{4j}.

Consider the final sum with 4 stochastic terms.
The 6-sums have zero covariances due to independent sampling.
The 5-sums also have zero covariances, because at least one of
v_{1}
or
v_{2}
needs to have different indices.
Within the 4-sum, the covariance is nonzero only for
j_{2}\neq j
. We require
i_{2}
to be equal to either
i
or
k
and
k_{2}
the other index.
Hence, by bounding covariances above by Cauchy-Schwarz,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}v_{1i}v_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(v_{1i}v_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{ik,-il}+G_{ij}F_{ij}G_{kl}F_{kl}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{ki,-kl}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(v_{1i}v_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)3!\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ij}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(v_{1i}v_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}G_{il}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}F_{ij}^{2}F_{il}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(v_{1i}v_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}G_{kl}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}F_{ij}^{2}F_{kl}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(v_{1i}v_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)3!\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ij}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}.

To obtain the first inequality, observe that once we have fixed 3
indices, there are
3!
permutations of the
v_{1i}v_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4j}

that we can calculate covariances for. They are all bounded above
by the variance. In the various combinations, we may have different
combinations of
G
and
F
, but they are bounded above by the expression.
To be precise, the 3-sum is:

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}+G_{ik}F_{ik}\check{M}_{ij,-ik}+G_{ji}F_{ji}\check{M}_{jk,-ji}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\left(G_{jk}F_{jk}\check{M}_{ji,-jk}+G_{ki}F_{ki}\check{M}_{kj,-ki}+G_{kj}F_{kj}\check{M}_{ki,-kj}\right).

Apply Cauchy-Schwarz to the sum and apply the commutative property
of summations to obtain the upper bound. For instance,

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}G_{jk}F_{jk}\check{M}_{ji,-jk}\right)^{2}\leq\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ij}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{jk}^{2}F_{jk}^{2}\check{M}_{ji,-jk}^{2}\right).

Then, observe that
\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{jk}^{2}F_{jk}^{2}\check{M}_{ji,-jk}^{2}=\sum_{j}\sum_{k\neq j}\sum_{i\neq j,k}G_{jk}^{2}F_{jk}^{2}\check{M}_{ji,-jk}^{2}\\
=\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ij}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}
.
Due to AS23 Equation (22),
\sum_{l}\check{M}_{il-ijk}^{2}=O(1)
,
so
\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ij}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}\leq C\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ij}^{2}\leq C\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}^{2}
.
Similarly,
\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}G_{kl}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}=O(1)\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}=O(1)\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}
,
which delivers the order required.

To deal with 3 stochastic terms,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}R_{1i}v_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}v_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{kj}F_{kj}\check{M}_{ki,-kj}R_{1k}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\textup{Var}\left(v_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{kj}F_{kj}\check{M}_{ki,-kj}R_{1k}\right)\left[\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{kj}F_{kj}\check{M}_{ki,-kj}R_{1k}+\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ki}F_{ki}\check{M}_{kj,-ki}R_{1k}\right]

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(v_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\sum_{l\neq i,j}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{kj}F_{kj}\check{M}_{ki,-kj}R_{1k}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,l}G_{kl}F_{kl}\check{M}_{ki,-kl}R_{1k}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\textup{Var}\left(v_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{kj}F_{kj}\check{M}_{ki,-kj}R_{1k}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\left[\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{kj}F_{kj}\check{M}_{ki,-kj}R_{1k}+\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ki}F_{ki}\check{M}_{kj,-ki}R_{1k}\right]

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(v_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{kj}F_{kj}\check{M}_{ki,-kj}R_{1k}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(v_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{kj}F_{kj}\check{M}_{ki,-kj}R_{1k}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(v_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{kj}F_{kj}\check{M}_{ki,-kj}R_{1k}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\textup{Var}\left(v_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{kj}F_{kj}\check{M}_{ki,-kj}R_{1k}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ki}F_{ki}\check{M}_{kj,-ki}R_{1k}\right)

To get the first inequality, observe that, if for
l\neq i,j
, we have
v_{2l}
instead of
V_{3l}V_{4l}
, the covariance must be 0.
We can then bound the order by using
Assumption

3
and
Lemma

3
.
Similarly,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}v_{1i}R_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}v_{1i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}R_{2k}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(v_{1i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}R_{2k}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\textup{Var}\left(v_{1i}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}R_{2k}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ji}F_{ji}\check{M}_{jk,-ij}R_{2k}\right).

since the expansion in the intermediate steps are entirely analogous.

Turning to the sum with two stochastic objects,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}R_{1i}R_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4j}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i}V_{3i}V_{4i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ji}F_{ji}\check{M}_{jk,-ij}R_{1j}R_{2k}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}V_{4i}\right)\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ji}F_{ji}\check{M}_{jk,-ij}R_{1j}R_{2k}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}V_{4i}\right)\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ji}F_{ji}\check{M}_{jk,-ij}R_{1j}R_{2k}\right)^{2}.

With these inequalities, applying
Assumption

3
suffices for the result.

Proof of
Lemma

4
(c)
. Expand the term:

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}V_{1i}V_{2i}V_{3j}V_{4l}=\sum_{i\neq j\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}V_{1i}V_{2i}\left(R_{3j}R_{4l}+R_{3j}v_{4l}+v_{3j}R_{4l}+v_{3j}v_{4l}\right).

With four stochastic objects,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}V_{1i}V_{2i}v_{3j}v_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}v_{3j}v_{4l}\right)\sum_{i\neq j\neq l}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}\neq i,j,l}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}G_{i_{2}j}F_{i_{2}j}\check{M}_{jl,-i_{2}j}+G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}G_{i_{2}l}F_{i_{2}l}\check{M}_{lj,-i_{2}l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}v_{3j}v_{4l}\right)3!\sum_{i\neq j\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ij}^{2}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}^{2}.

Simplifying the first line,

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq l}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}\neq i,j,l}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}G_{i_{2}j}F_{i_{2}j}\check{M}_{jl,-i_{2}j}+G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}G_{i_{2}l}F_{i_{2}l}\check{M}_{lj,-i_{2}l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq l\neq i_{2}}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}G_{i_{2}j}^{2}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq l\neq i_{2}}^{n}F_{ij}^{2}F_{i_{2}j}^{2}\check{M}_{jl,-i_{2}j}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq l\neq i_{2}}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}G_{i_{2}l}^{2}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq l\neq i_{2}}^{n}F_{ij}^{2}F_{i_{2}l}^{2}\check{M}_{lj,-i_{2}j}^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

These terms have the required order due to a proof analogous to
Lemma

4
(b). Next,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}V_{1i}V_{2i}R_{3j}v_{4l}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}V_{1i}V_{2i}v_{4j}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{lj,-il}R_{3l}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}v_{4j}\right)\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{lj,-il}R_{3l}\right)\left[\sum_{l\neq i,j}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{lj,-il}R_{3l}+\sum_{l\neq i,j}G_{jl}F_{jl}\check{M}_{li,-jl}R_{3l}\right]

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}v_{4j}\right)\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{i_{2}\neq i,j}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{lj,-il}R_{3l}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i_{2},l}G_{kl}F_{kl}\check{M}_{ki_{2},-kl}R_{1k}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}v_{4j}\right)\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{l\neq i,j}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{lj,-il}R_{3l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}v_{4j}\right)\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{lj,-il}R_{3l}\right)\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j}G_{jl}F_{jl}\check{M}_{li,-jl}R_{3l}\right).

Further,
\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}V_{1i}V_{2i}v_{3j}R_{4l}\right)
can be bounded by a similar argument.
Turning to the sum with two stochastic objects,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}V_{1i}V_{2i}R_{3j}R_{4l}\right)=\sum_{i}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}\right)\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{l\neq i,j}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ij}R_{3j}R_{4l}\right)^{2}.

These inequalities suffice for the result due to
Assumption

3
.

Proof of
Lemma

4
(d).

Expand the term:

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}V_{1i}V_{2k}V_{3j}V_{4l}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}V_{1i}R_{2k}\left(R_{3j}R_{4l}+R_{3j}v_{4l}+v_{3j}R_{4l}+v_{3j}v_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}V_{1i}v_{2k}\left(R_{3j}R_{4l}+R_{3j}v_{4l}+v_{3j}R_{4l}+v_{3j}v_{4l}\right).

Consider the
v_{2k}
line first.
We only have the 4-sum to contend with.
For 5-sum and above, at least one of the errors can be factored out as a zero expectation.
Hence, by using Cauchy-Schwarz and the same argument as above,

\displaystyle\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{3j}v_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j,k,l}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{3j}v_{4l}\right)4!\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ij}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ij}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}\leq C\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ij}^{2}\leq C\left(\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}F_{ij}^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

By using the same expansion step as before,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{3j}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}R_{4l}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{3j}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}R_{4l}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq i_{2}}^{n}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}G_{i_{2}j}F_{i_{2}j}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{i_{2}k,-ij}+G_{ij}F_{ij}G_{i_{2}k}F_{i_{2}k}\check{M}_{ij,-ik}\check{M}_{i_{2}j,-ik}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{3j}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}R_{4l}\right)\right)3!\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ij}^{2}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}.

The
\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq i_{2}}^{n}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}G_{i_{2}j}F_{i_{2}j}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{i_{2}k,-ij}+G_{ij}F_{ij}G_{i_{2}k}F_{i_{2}k}\check{M}_{ij,-ik}\check{M}_{i_{2}j,-ik}\right)

term has the required order due to the same argument as the proof
of
Lemma

4
(b). Next,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}V_{1i}v_{2k}R_{3j}v_{4l}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{ik,-il}\check{M}_{lj,-ilk}R_{3l}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}\neq i,j,k}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{ik,-il}\check{M}_{lj,-ilk}R_{3l}\right)\left(\sum_{l\neq i_{2},j,k}G_{i_{2}l}F_{i_{2}l}\check{M}_{i_{2}k,-i_{2}l}\check{M}_{lj,-i_{2}lk}R_{3l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}\neq i,j,k}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{ik,-il}\check{M}_{lj,-ilk}R_{3l}\right)\left(\sum_{l\neq i_{2},j,k}G_{i_{2}l}F_{i_{2}l}\check{M}_{i_{2}j,-i_{2}l}\check{M}_{lk,-i_{2}lj}R_{3l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\right)3!\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{ik,-il}\check{M}_{lj,-ilk}R_{3l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\right)\sum_{k}\sum_{j\neq k}\left(\sum_{i\neq k,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{ik,-il}\check{M}_{lj,-ilk}R_{3l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt-\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\right)\sum_{k}\sum_{j\neq k}\sum_{i\neq k,j}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{ik,-il}\check{M}_{lj,-ilk}R_{3l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\right)\sum_{k}\sum_{j\neq k}\left(\sum_{i\neq k,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{ik,-il}\check{M}_{lj,-ilk}R_{3l}\right)\left(\sum_{i\neq k,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{ij,-il}\check{M}_{lk,-ilj}R_{3l}\right)

\displaystyle-\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\right)\sum_{k}\sum_{j\neq k}\sum_{i\neq k,j}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{ik,-il}\check{M}_{lj,-ilk}R_{3l}\right)\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{ij,-il}\check{M}_{lk,-ilj}R_{3l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\right)3!\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}^{2}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{il}\check{M}_{ik,-il}\check{M}_{lj,-ilk}R_{3l}\right)^{2}.

The first term in the
v_{2k}
line is then:

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}V_{1i}v_{2k}R_{3j}R_{4l}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ij,-ik}V_{1i}v_{2j}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{kl,-ijk}R_{3k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ij,-ik}\check{M}_{kl,-ijk}R_{3k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ij,-ik}\check{M}_{kl,-ijk}R_{3k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\left(G_{ji}F_{ji}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ji,-jk}\check{M}_{kl,-ijk}R_{3k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j\neq i_{2}}^{n}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ij,-ik}\check{M}_{kl,-ijk}R_{3k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\left(G_{i_{2}j}F_{i_{2}j}\sum_{k\neq i_{2},j}\sum_{l\neq i_{2},j,k}\check{M}_{i_{2}j,-i_{2}k}\check{M}_{kl,-i_{2}jk}R_{3k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ij,-ik}\check{M}_{kl,-ijk}R_{3k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ij,-ik}\check{M}_{kl,-ijk}R_{3k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\left(G_{ji}F_{ji}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ji,-jk}\check{M}_{kl,-ijk}R_{3k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2j}\right)\sum_{j}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ij,-ik}\check{M}_{kl,-ijk}R_{3k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt-\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{2j}\right)\sum_{j}\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ij,-ik}\check{M}_{kl,-ijk}R_{3k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}.

Now, we turn back to the
R_{2k}
expression to complete the proof:

\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}V_{1i}R_{2k}\left(R_{3j}R_{4l}+R_{3j}v_{4l}+v_{3j}R_{4l}+v_{3j}v_{4l}\right).

Consider the term with three stochastic terms first, and simplify it using the same strategy as before:

\displaystyle\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}V_{1i}R_{2k}v_{3j}v_{4l}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}V_{1i}v_{3j}v_{4k}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{il,-ij}\check{M}_{jk,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{3j}v_{4k}\right)\bigg(\sum_{k\neq j}^{n}\left(\sum_{i\neq k,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{il,-ij}\check{M}_{jk,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt-\sum_{k\neq j}^{n}\sum_{i\neq k,j}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{il,-ij}\check{M}_{jk,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{3j}v_{4k}\right)\sum_{k}\sum_{j\neq k}\left(\sum_{i\neq k,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{il,-ij}\check{M}_{jk,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)\left(\sum_{i\neq k,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ik}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ik}\check{M}_{kj,-ikl}R_{2l}\right)

\displaystyle-\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{3j}v_{4k}\right)\sum_{k}\sum_{j\neq k}\sum_{i\neq k,j}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{il,-ij}\check{M}_{jk,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ik}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ik}\check{M}_{kj,-ikl}R_{2l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{3j}v_{4k}\right)3!\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{il,-ij}\check{M}_{jk,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)^{2}.

Next,

\displaystyle\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}V_{1i}R_{2k}v_{3j}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{3j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{3j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)\left(G_{ji}F_{ji}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{jk,-ij}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{3j}\right)\sum_{j}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}G_{ij}F_{ij}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle-\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}v_{3j}\right)\sum_{j\neq i}^{n}\left(G_{ij}F_{ij}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}.

Finally,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}V_{1i}R_{2k}R_{3j}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt=\sum_{i}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}\right)\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}R_{2k}R_{3j}R_{4l}\right)^{2}.

∎

Proof of
Lemma

5
.

Proof of
Lemma

5
(a).

Expand the term:

\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}V_{1j}V_{2k}V_{3i}V_{4i}=\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}V_{3i}V_{4i}\left(R_{1j}R_{2k}+R_{1j}v_{2k}+v_{1j}R_{2k}+v_{1j}v_{2k}\right).

With four stochastic objects,

\displaystyle Var\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}V_{3i}V_{4i}v_{1j}v_{2k}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}V_{4i}v_{1j}v_{2k}\right)\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}\neq i,j,k}\left(G_{ij}F_{ik}G_{i_{2}j}F_{i_{2}k}+G_{ij}F_{ik}G_{i_{2}k}F_{i_{2}j}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{1i}V_{2i}v_{3j}v_{4l}\right)3!\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ik}^{2}.

Observe that, due to
Assumption

3
(a),

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}G_{lj}F_{lk}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\neq k}^{n}\left(\sum_{i\neq j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\right)\left(\sum_{l\neq j,k}G_{lj}F_{lk}-G_{ij}F_{ik}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\neq k}^{n}\left(\sum_{i\neq j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\right)^{2}-\sum_{j\neq k\neq i}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ik}^{2}

has the required order, which suffices for the bound. Next,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}V_{3i}V_{4i}R_{1j}v_{2k}\right)

\displaystyle=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}F_{ij}V_{3i}V_{4i}v_{2j}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ik}R_{1k}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}V_{4i}v_{2j}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}F_{ij}G_{ik}R_{1k}\right)\left[\sum_{k\neq i,j}F_{ij}G_{ik}R_{1k}+\sum_{k\neq i,j}F_{ji}G_{jk}R_{1k}\right]

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}V_{4i}v_{2j}\right)\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{i_{2}\neq i,j}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}F_{ij}G_{ik}R_{1k}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i_{2},l}F_{i_{2}j}G_{i_{2}k}R_{1k}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}V_{4i}v_{2j}\right)\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}F_{ij}G_{ik}R_{1k}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}V_{4i}v_{2j}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}F_{ij}G_{ik}R_{1k}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}F_{ji}G_{jk}R_{1k}\right).

Similarly,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}V_{3i}V_{4i}v_{1j}R_{2k}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}V_{3i}V_{4i}v_{1j}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ij}F_{ik}R_{2k}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}V_{4i}v_{1j}\right)\sum_{i}\left(\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ij}F_{ik}R_{2k}\right)^{2}+\sum_{j\neq i}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ij}F_{ik}R_{2k}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ji}F_{jk}R_{2k}\right)\right).

Turning to the sum with two stochastic objects,

Var

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}V_{3i}V_{4i}R_{1j}R_{2k}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i}V_{3i}V_{4i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ij}F_{ik}R_{1j}R_{2k}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}V_{4i}\right)\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}G_{ij}F_{ik}R_{1j}R_{2k}\right)^{2}.

Proof of
Lemma

5
(b).

Decompose the term:

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}V_{1j}V_{2k}V_{3i}V_{4l}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}V_{3i}R_{1j}\left(R_{2k}R_{4l}+R_{2k}v_{4l}+v_{2k}R_{4l}+v_{2k}v_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}V_{3i}v_{1j}\left(R_{2k}R_{4l}+R_{2k}v_{4l}+v_{2k}R_{4l}+v_{2k}v_{4l}\right).

Consider the
v_{1j}
line first.

\displaystyle\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{2k}v_{4l}\right)\leq\max_{i,j,k,l}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{2k}v_{4l}\right)4!\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ik}^{2}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}^{2}.

Next, by using the same expansion and simplification steps as before,

\displaystyle\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{2k}R_{4l}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{2k}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{2k}\right)\sum_{k}\sum_{j\neq k}\left(\left(\sum_{i\neq j,k}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{4l}\right)^{2}-\sum_{i\neq j,k}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{4l}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{2k}\right)\sum_{k}\sum_{j\neq k}\left(\sum_{i\neq j,k}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{4l}\right)\left(\sum_{i\neq j,k}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ik}F_{ij}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle-\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{2k}\right)\sum_{k}\sum_{j\neq k}\sum_{i\neq j,k}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{4l}\right)\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ik}F_{ij}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{2k}\right)3!\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ik}^{2}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{4l}\right)^{2}

and

\displaystyle\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}V_{3i}v_{1j}R_{2k}v_{4l}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{4k}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ik,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{4k}\right)\sum_{k}\sum_{j\neq k}\left(\left(\sum_{i\neq j,k}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{ik,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)^{2}-\sum_{i\neq j,k}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{ik,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{2k}\right)\sum_{k}\sum_{j\neq k}\left(\sum_{i\neq j,k}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{ik,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)\left(\sum_{i\neq j,k}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ik}F_{ij}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{2l}\right)

\displaystyle-\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{2k}\right)\sum_{k}\sum_{j\neq k}\sum_{i\neq j,k}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{ik,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ik}F_{ij}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{2l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{1j}v_{4k}\right)3!\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{ij}^{2}F_{ik}^{2}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ik,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)^{2}

with
\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ik,-ijl}R_{2l}\right)^{2}\leq C
.
Finally,

\displaystyle\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}V_{3i}v_{1j}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}G_{ij}V_{3i}v_{1j}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{1j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}G_{ij}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{1j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ji}F_{jk}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{1j}\right)\sum_{j}\left(\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}-\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}\right).

Now, return to the
R_{1j}
line:
\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}V_{3i}R_{1j}\left(R_{2k}R_{4l}+R_{2k}v_{4l}+v_{2k}R_{4l}+v_{2k}v_{4l}\right)
,
so

\displaystyle\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}V_{3i}R_{1j}v_{2k}v_{4l}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}G_{il}F_{ik}V_{3i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}\check{M}_{ij,-ilk}R_{1l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\right)\left(\sum_{j}\sum_{k\neq j}\left(\sum_{i\neq j,k}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{ik}\check{M}_{ij,-ilk}R_{1l}\right)^{2}-\sum_{j}\sum_{k\neq j}\sum_{i\neq j,k}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{ik}\check{M}_{ij,-ilk}R_{1l}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\right)\sum_{j}\sum_{k\neq j}\left(\sum_{i\neq j,k}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{ik}\check{M}_{ij,-ilk}R_{1l}\right)\left(\sum_{i\neq j,k}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ilj}R_{1l}\right)

\displaystyle-\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\right)\sum_{j}\sum_{k\neq j}\sum_{i\neq j,k}\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{ik}\check{M}_{ij,-ilk}R_{1l}\right)\left(\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}F_{ij}\check{M}_{ik,-ilj}R_{1l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j,k}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{2k}v_{4j}\right)3!\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}^{n}\left(F_{ik}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{il}\check{M}_{ij,-ilk}R_{1l}\right)^{2},

and

\displaystyle\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}V_{3i}R_{1j}v_{2k}R_{4l}\right)=\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}F_{ij}V_{3i}v_{2j}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{1k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{2j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}F_{ij}G_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{1k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{2j}\right)\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}F_{ij}G_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{1k}R_{4l}\right)\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}F_{ij}G_{jk}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}R_{1k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle+\max_{i,j}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}v_{2j}\right)\sum_{j}\left(\left(\sum_{i\neq j}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}F_{ij}G_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{1k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}-\sum_{i\neq j}\left(\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}F_{ij}G_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{1k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}\right).

The
\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}V_{3i}R_{1j}R_{2k}v_{4l}

term is symmetric, because it does not matter which
R_{m}
we use.
Finally,

\displaystyle\textup{Var}\left(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq l}^{n}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}V_{3i}R_{1j}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\textup{Var}\left(V_{3i}\right)\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i,j}\sum_{l\neq i,j,k}G_{ij}F_{ik}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}R_{1j}R_{2k}R_{4l}\right)^{2}.

∎

Proof of
Lemma

6
.

The proof of
Lemma

6
is entirely analogous to Lemmas
4
and
5
just that
G_{ji}
is used in place of
G_{ij}
.
∎

E.2
Proofs for Appendix D

E.2.1
Proofs for Propositions in Appendix D

Proof of
Proposition

2
.

Let

\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\Pi_{Y}\\
\Pi\end{array}\right):=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{1/2}\pi_{Y}\\
\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{1/2}\pi\end{array}\right).

With this definition,
\left(\pi_{Y}^{\prime}Z^{\prime}Z\pi_{Y},\pi^{\prime}Z^{\prime}Z\pi_{Y},\pi^{\prime}Z^{\prime}Z\pi\right)=\left(\Pi_{Y}^{\prime}\Pi_{Y},\Pi_{Y}^{\prime}\Pi,\Pi^{\prime}\Pi\right)
, and

\left(\begin{array}[]{c}s_{1}\\
s_{2}\end{array}\right)\sim N\left(\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\Pi_{Y}\\
\Pi\end{array}\right),\Omega\otimes I_{K}\right).

Split
s_{1}
and
s_{2}
into the
\Pi
component and a random
normal component:
s_{1k}=\Pi_{Yk}+z_{1k}
and
s_{2k}=\Pi_{k}+z_{2k}
.
Then, for all
k
,

\left(\begin{array}[]{c}z_{1k}\\
z_{2k}\end{array}\right)\sim N\left(\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\end{array}\right),\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}&\omega_{\zeta\eta}\\
\omega_{\zeta\eta}&\omega_{\eta\eta}\end{array}\right]\right),\text{ and }

\displaystyle\left(\begin{array}[]{c}s_{1}^{\prime}s_{1}\\
s_{1}^{\prime}s_{2}\\
s_{2}^{\prime}s_{2}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\sum_{k}s_{1k}^{2}\\
\sum_{k}s_{1k}s_{2k}\\
\sum_{k}s_{2k}^{2}\end{array}\right)=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\sum_{k}\left(\Pi_{Yk}+z_{1k}\right)^{2}\\
\sum_{k}\left(\Pi_{Yk}+z_{1k}\right)\left(\Pi_{k}+z_{2k}\right)\\
\sum_{k}\left(\Pi_{k}+z_{2k}\right)^{2}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}^{2}+2\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}z_{1k}+\sum_{k}z_{1k}^{2}\\
\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}\Pi_{k}+\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}z_{2k}+\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}z_{1k}+\sum_{k}z_{1k}z_{2k}\\
\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}^{2}+2\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}z_{2k}+\sum_{k}z_{2k}^{2}\end{array}\right).

Under the assumption,
\Pi^{\prime}\Pi/\sqrt{K}\rightarrow C_{S}
, so
\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}^{2}\rightarrow C_{S}
.
By applying the Lindeberg CLT due to bounded moments,

\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}z_{1k}\\
\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}z_{1k}\\
\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}z_{2k}\\
\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}z_{2k}\\
\sum_{k}z_{1k}z_{2k}\\
\sum_{k}z_{2k}^{2}\\
\sum_{k}z_{1k}^{2}\end{array}\right)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle a}}{{\sim}}N\left(\left(\begin{array}[]{c}0\\
0\\
0\\
0\\
\sqrt{K}\omega_{\zeta\eta}\\
\sqrt{K}\omega_{\eta\eta}\\
\sqrt{K}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}\end{array}\right),V\right),

where
V
is some variance matrix.
By assumption,
\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}\Pi_{k}\rightarrow C_{Y}
and
\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}^{2}\rightarrow C_{YY}
, so

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}s_{1}^{\prime}s_{1}\\
s_{1}^{\prime}s_{2}\\
s_{2}^{\prime}s_{2}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}^{2}+2\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}z_{1k}+\sum_{k}z_{1k}^{2}\\
\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}\Pi_{k}+\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}z_{2k}+\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}z_{1k}+\sum_{k}z_{1k}z_{2k}\\
\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}^{2}+2\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}z_{2k}+\sum_{k}z_{2k}^{2}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle a}}{{\sim}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}C_{YY}\\
C_{Y}\\
C\end{array}\right)+A\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}z_{1k}\\
\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}z_{1k}\\
\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}z_{2k}\\
\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}z_{2k}\\
\sum_{k}z_{1k}z_{2k}\\
\sum_{k}z_{2k}^{2}\\
\sum_{k}z_{1k}^{2}\end{array}\right),\text{ where }

A=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccccccc}0&2&0&0&0&0&1\\
1&0&1&0&1&0&0\\
0&0&0&2&0&1&0\end{array}\right).

This means:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}s_{1}^{\prime}s_{1}\\
s_{1}^{\prime}s_{2}\\
s_{2}^{\prime}s_{2}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle a}}{{\sim}}N\left(\left(\begin{array}[]{c}C_{YY}+\sqrt{K}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}\\
C_{Y}+\sqrt{K}\omega_{\zeta\eta}\\
C+\sqrt{K}\omega_{\eta\eta}\end{array}\right),AVA^{\prime}\right).

Let
\Sigma=AVA^{\prime}
to obtain the result as stated.
To derive
\Sigma
explicitly, I derive
V
by applying the Isserlis’ Theroem.
As a special case of the Isserlis’ Theorem for
X
’s that are multivariate normal and mean zero,

E\left[X_{1}X_{2}X_{3}X_{4}\right]=E\left[X_{1}X_{2}\right]E\left[X_{3}X_{4}\right]+E\left[X_{1}X_{3}\right]E\left[X_{2}X_{4}\right]+E\left[X_{1}X_{4}\right]E\left[X_{2}X_{3}\right].

Another corrolary is that if
n
is odd, then there is no such pairing, so the moment is always zero.
Hence,

\displaystyle E\left[z_{1k}^{2}z_{2k}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=E\left[z_{1k}^{2}\right]E\left[z_{2k}^{2}\right]+2E\left[z_{1k}z_{2k}\right]E\left[z_{1k}z_{2k}\right]=\omega_{\zeta\zeta}\omega_{\eta\eta}+2\omega_{\zeta\eta}^{2},\text{ and }

\displaystyle\textup{Var}\left(z_{1k}z_{2k}\right)

\displaystyle=\omega_{\zeta\zeta}\omega_{\eta\eta}+\omega_{\zeta\eta}^{2}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\textup{Var}\left(z_{2k}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=E\left[z_{2k}^{4}\right]-\omega_{\eta\eta}^{2}=3\omega_{\eta\eta}^{2}-\omega_{\eta\eta}^{2}=2\omega_{\eta\eta}^{2},

\displaystyle Cov\left(z_{1k},z_{1k}z_{2k}\right)

\displaystyle=E\left[z_{1k}^{2}z_{2k}\right]-E\left[z_{1k}\right]E\left[z_{1k}z_{2k}\right]=0,

\displaystyle Cov\left(z_{1k}^{2},z_{1k}z_{2k}\right)

\displaystyle=E\left[z_{1k}^{3}z_{2k}\right]-E\left[z_{1k}^{2}\right]E\left[z_{1k}z_{2k}\right]

\displaystyle=3\omega_{\zeta\eta}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}-\omega_{\zeta\zeta}\omega_{\zeta\eta}=2\omega_{\zeta\eta}\omega_{\zeta\zeta},

\displaystyle Cov\left(z_{1k}^{2},z_{2k}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=E\left[z_{1k}^{2}z_{2k}^{2}\right]-\omega_{\zeta\zeta}\omega_{\eta\eta}=2\omega_{\zeta\eta}^{2},\text{ and }

V=\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}V_{11}&0\\
0&V_{22}\end{array}\right],\text{ where }

V_{11}=\left[\begin{array}[]{ccccccc}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}^{2}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}&\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}\Pi_{Yk}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}&\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}\Pi_{Yk}\omega_{\zeta\eta}&\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}^{2}\omega_{\zeta\eta}\\
.&\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}^{2}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}&\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}^{2}\omega_{\zeta\eta}&\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}\Pi_{Yk}\omega_{\zeta\eta}\\
.&.&\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}^{2}\omega_{\eta\eta}&\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}\Pi_{Yk}\omega_{\eta\eta}\\
.&.&.&\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}^{2}\omega_{\eta\eta}\end{array}\right],

V_{22}=\left[\begin{array}[]{ccccccc}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}\omega_{\eta\eta}+\omega_{\zeta\eta}^{2}&2\omega_{\zeta\eta}\omega_{\eta\eta}&2\omega_{\zeta\eta}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}\\
.&2\omega_{\eta\eta}^{2}&2\omega_{\zeta\eta}^{2}\\
.&.&2\omega_{\zeta\zeta}^{2}\end{array}\right].

If
\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}^{2}\rightarrow 0,\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{k}\Pi_{Yk}\rightarrow 0,\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\Pi_{Yk}^{2}\rightarrow 0
under weak identification, then we obtain the
\Sigma
expression stated in the proposition.
∎

Proof of
Proposition

3
.

Use
n_{q}^{Q}
and
n_{w}^{W}
to denote the number of observations in the instrument and covariate groups respectively, so

\displaystyle\mu_{3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}R_{i}R_{j}=\sum_{q}\frac{n_{q}^{Q}}{n_{q}^{Q}-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{q}^{Q}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{q}^{Q},j\neq i}\frac{1}{n_{q}^{Q}}R_{i}R_{j}-\sum_{w}\frac{n_{w}^{W}}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W},j\neq i}\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}}R_{i}R_{j}

\displaystyle=\sum_{q}\frac{1}{n_{q}^{Q}-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{q}^{Q}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{q}^{Q},j\neq i}R_{i}R_{j}-\sum_{w}\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W},j\neq i}R_{i}R_{j}

\displaystyle=\sum_{w}\left(\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\frac{1}{n_{q}^{Q}-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{q}^{Q}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{q}^{Q},j\neq i}R_{i}R_{j}-\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W},j\neq i}R_{i}R_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{w}\left(\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\frac{n_{q}^{Q}\left(n_{q}^{Q}-1\right)}{n_{q}^{Q}-1}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W},j\neq i}R_{i}R_{j}\right).

Considering the second term,

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W},j\neq i}R_{i}R_{j}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W},j\neq i}\left(\pi_{q(i)}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{q(j)}+\gamma_{w}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{q}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W},j\neq i}\left(\pi_{q(i)}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{q(j)}+\gamma_{w}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}n_{q}^{Q}\left(n_{q}^{Q}-1\right)\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)^{2}+\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{q}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W},j\neq i}\left(\pi_{q(i)}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{q(j)}+\gamma_{w}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}n_{q}^{Q}\left(n_{q}^{Q}-1\right)\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)^{2}+\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}\neq q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{q^{\prime}}+\gamma_{w}\right).

Since

\displaystyle\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}n_{q}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}n_{q}^{Q}\left(n_{q}^{Q}-1\right)\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)^{2}=\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}n_{q}^{Q}\left(\frac{n_{w}^{W}-n_{q}^{Q}}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\right)\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)^{2},

and
n_{w}^{W}=\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}n_{q}^{Q}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mu_{3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{w}\left(\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}n_{q}^{Q}\left(\frac{n_{w}^{W}-n_{q}^{Q}}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\right)\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}\neq q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{q^{\prime}}+\gamma_{w}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{w}\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\left(\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}\neq q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)^{2}-\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}\neq q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{q^{\prime}}+\gamma_{w}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{w}\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\left(\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}\neq q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q^{\prime}}\right)\right).

Then, observe that

\displaystyle\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}\neq q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma\right)\left(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}<q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma\right)\left(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q^{\prime}}\right)+\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}>q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma\right)\left(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}<q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma\right)\left(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q^{\prime}}\right)-\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}<q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q^{\prime}}+\gamma\right)\left(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}<q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q^{\prime}}\right)\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma-\pi_{q^{\prime}}-\gamma\right)=\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}<q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q^{\prime}}\right)^{2},

where the second equality switches the indices of
q
and
q^{\prime}
in the second element. Hence,

\displaystyle\mu_{3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{w}\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\left(\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}<q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right)\geq 0.

Analogously,

\displaystyle\mu_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{w}\left(\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\frac{1}{n_{q}^{Q}-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{q}^{Q}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{q}^{Q},j\neq i}R_{i}R_{Yj}-\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{w}^{W},j\neq i}R_{i}R_{Yj}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{w}\left(\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}n_{q}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{Yq}+\gamma_{w}\right)-\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}n_{q}^{Q}\left(n_{q}^{Q}-1\right)\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{Yq}+\gamma_{w}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\sum_{w}\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}\neq q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{Yq^{\prime}}+\gamma_{w}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{w}\bigg(\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}n_{q}^{Q}\left(\frac{n_{w}^{W}-n_{q}^{Q}}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\right)\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{Yq}+\gamma_{w}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}\neq q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{Yq^{\prime}}+\gamma_{w}\right)\bigg)

\displaystyle=\sum_{w}\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\left(\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}\neq q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}+\gamma_{w}\right)\left(\pi_{Yq}-\pi_{Yq^{\prime}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{w}\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\left(\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}<q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{q}-\pi_{q^{\prime}}\right)\left(\pi_{Yq}-\pi_{Yq^{\prime}}\right)\right),

and

\mu_{1}=\sum_{w}\frac{1}{n_{w}^{W}-1}\left(\sum_{q\in\mathcal{M}_{w}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{M}_{w},q^{\prime}<q}n_{q}^{Q}n_{q^{\prime}}^{Q}\left(\pi_{Yq}-\pi_{Yq^{\prime}}\right)^{2}\right)\geq 0.

∎

Proof of
Proposition

4
.

Fix any alternative
\left(\pi^{A},\pi_{Y}^{A}\right)\in\mathcal{S}
with a corresponding
\left(\mu_{1}^{A},\mu_{2}^{A},\mu_{3}^{A}\right)
.
Due to the restriction in
\mathcal{S}
,

\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mu_{1}^{H}\\
\mu_{2}^{H}\\
\mu_{3}^{H}\end{array}\right)=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mu_{1}^{A}-\frac{\sigma_{12}}{\sigma_{22}}\mu_{2}^{A}\\
0\\
\mu_{3}^{A}-\frac{\sigma_{23}}{\sigma_{22}}\mu_{2}^{A}\end{array}\right)

is in the null space.
The Neyman-Pearson test for
\mu^{H}
against
\mu^{A}
rejects large values of:

\log\frac{dN\left(\mu^{A},\Sigma\right)}{dN\left(\mu^{H},\Sigma\right)}=\frac{\mu_{2}^{A}}{\sigma_{22}}X_{2}-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\left(\mu_{2}^{A}\right)^{2}}{\sigma_{22}}.

Hence, the most powerful test rejects large values of
X_{2}
, which
is what LM does.
By
Lehmann and Romano (2005)
Theorem 3.8.1(i), since LM is valid for any distribution in the null space (by
Theorem

1
) and it is most powerful for some distribution in the null space, LM is most powerful for testing the composite null against the given alternative
\left(\pi^{A},\pi_{Y}^{A}\right)
.
∎

Proof of
Proposition

5
.

The first two are straightforward:
C_{S}=\mu_{3}/\left(c-1\right)
and
\beta=\mu_{2}/\mu_{3}
imply
\mu_{3}=\left(c-1\right)C_{S}
and
\mu_{2}=\left(c-1\right)C_{S}\beta
.
For
\mu_{1}
, observe that:

\displaystyle h

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\frac{1}{c-1}\left(\mu_{1}-\frac{\mu_{2}^{2}}{\mu_{3}}\right)}=\sqrt{\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\left(\mu_{1}-C_{S}\beta^{2}\right)},\text{ and }

\displaystyle C_{H}=\sqrt{K}h^{2}

\displaystyle=\mu_{1}/\left(c-1\right)-C_{S}\beta^{2},\text{ so }

\displaystyle\left(c-1\right)\left(C_{S}\beta^{2}+C_{H}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(c-1\right)\left(C_{S}\beta^{2}+\mu_{1}/\left(c-1\right)-C_{S}\beta^{2}\right)=\mu_{1}

as required.
Next, since
\sigma_{vv}=\sqrt{\frac{\sigma_{33}c}{2\left(c-1\right)}}
,
\sigma_{33}=2\frac{c-1}{c}\sigma_{vv}^{2}
is immediate.
Similarly, with
\sigma_{\varepsilon v}=\frac{1}{\sigma_{vv}}\left(\frac{\sigma_{23}c}{2\left(c-1\right)}-\sigma_{vv}^{2}\beta\right)
,
\sigma_{23}=2\frac{c-1}{c}\sigma_{vv}\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)
.
From these two expressions, we can observe that:

\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)^{2}=\frac{c}{2\left(c-1\right)}\frac{\sigma_{23}^{2}}{\sigma_{33}}.

To obtain an expression for
\sigma_{22}
, rearrange
\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}=\frac{1}{\sigma_{vv}}\frac{c}{c-1}\left(\sigma_{22}-\frac{\sigma_{23}^{2}}{\sigma_{33}}\right)+\frac{\sigma_{\varepsilon v}^{2}}{\sigma_{vv}}\geq 0
:

\displaystyle\sigma_{22}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma_{23}^{2}}{\sigma_{33}}+\frac{c-1}{c}\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}\sigma_{vv}-\sigma_{\varepsilon v}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{c-1}{c}\left(\sigma_{vv}\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)+\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)^{2}\right)+o(1),

where the final step uses
\sigma_{\xi\xi}=h/\sigma_{vv}
.
This expression for
\sigma_{22}
is of the form required in
Lemma

8
. Then,

\displaystyle det\left(\Sigma_{SF}\right)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}\sigma_{\xi\xi}\sigma_{vv}-\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}h^{2}-\sigma_{\varepsilon\xi}^{2}\sigma_{vv}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon\xi}\sigma_{\varepsilon v}h-\sigma_{\xi\xi}\sigma_{\varepsilon v}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}\sigma_{\xi\xi}\sigma_{vv}-\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}h^{2}-\sigma_{\xi\xi}\sigma_{\varepsilon v}^{2}=\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}h-\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}h^{2}-h\frac{\sigma_{\varepsilon v}^{2}}{\sigma_{vv}};\text{ and }

\displaystyle det\left(\Sigma_{SF}\right)/h

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}-\frac{\sigma_{\varepsilon v}^{2}}{\sigma_{vv}}-\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}h=\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}-\frac{\sigma_{\varepsilon v}^{2}}{\sigma_{vv}}+o(1).

An analogous argument holds for
\sigma_{\xi vk}=-h
. From the
\sigma_{22}
equation,
\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}-\frac{\sigma_{\varepsilon v}^{2}}{\sigma_{vv}}=\frac{c}{c-1}\left(\sigma_{22}-\frac{\sigma_{23}^{2}}{\sigma_{33}}\right)\geq 0
, which delivers the result that
det\left(\Sigma_{SF}\right)/h\rightarrow C_{D}\geq 0
.
∎

Proof of
Proposition

6
.

The
A
expressions can be written as:

\displaystyle A_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}\sum_{l\neq k}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}G_{ij}X_{j}G_{ik}X_{k}\left(Y_{i}Y_{l}-X_{i}Y_{l}\beta_{0}-Y_{i}X_{l}\beta_{0}+X_{i}X_{l}\beta_{0}^{2}\right);

\displaystyle A_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}\sum_{l\neq k}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}G_{ij}X_{j}G_{ki}X_{l}\left(Y_{i}Y_{k}-X_{i}Y_{k}\beta_{0}-Y_{i}X_{k}\beta_{0}+X_{i}X_{k}\beta_{0}^{2}\right);

\displaystyle A_{3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}\sum_{l\neq k}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}X_{l}G_{ji}G_{ki}X_{i}\left(Y_{j}Y_{k}-X_{j}Y_{k}\beta_{0}-Y_{j}X_{k}\beta_{0}+X_{j}X_{k}\beta_{0}^{2}\right);

\displaystyle A_{4}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq j}\sum_{l\neq i,k}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}G_{ji}^{2}X_{i}X_{k}\left(Y_{j}Y_{l}-X_{j}Y_{l}\beta_{0}-Y_{j}X_{l}\beta_{0}+X_{j}X_{l}\beta_{0}^{2}\right);\text{ and }

\displaystyle A_{5}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq j}\sum_{l\neq i,k}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}G_{ij}G_{ji}X_{k}X_{l}\left(Y_{i}Y_{j}-X_{i}Y_{j}\beta_{0}-Y_{i}X_{j}\beta_{0}+X_{i}X_{j}\beta_{0}^{2}\right).

Since these terms have a quadratic form, the variance estimator is also quadratic in
\beta_{0}
, i.e.,

\hat{V}_{LM}=B_{0}+B_{1}\beta_{0}+B_{2}\beta_{0}^{2},

where the
B
’s can be worked out by collecting the expressions above.
For instance,

\displaystyle B_{0}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}\sum_{l\neq k}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}G_{ij}X_{j}G_{ik}X_{k}Y_{i}Y_{l}+2\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}\sum_{l\neq k}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}G_{ij}X_{j}G_{ki}X_{l}Y_{i}Y_{k}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}\sum_{l\neq k}\check{M}_{il,-ijk}X_{l}G_{ji}G_{ki}X_{i}Y_{j}Y_{k}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq j}\sum_{l\neq i,k}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}G_{ji}^{2}X_{i}X_{k}Y_{j}Y_{l}-\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq j}\sum_{l\neq i,k}\check{M}_{ik,-ij}\check{M}_{jl,-ijk}G_{ij}G_{ji}X_{k}X_{l}Y_{i}Y_{j}

B_{1}
and
B_{2}
are analogous by collecting the coefficients on
\beta_{0},\beta_{0}^{2}
from expressions
A_{1}
to
A_{5}
.
The test does not reject:

\displaystyle\frac{\left(KT_{YX}-KT_{XX}\beta_{0}\right)^{2}}{B_{0}+B_{1}\beta_{0}+B_{2}\beta_{0}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq q\Leftrightarrow\left(KT_{XX}^{2}-qB_{2}\right)\beta_{0}^{2}-\left(2KT_{YX}T_{XX}+qB_{1}\right)\beta_{0}+\left(KT_{YX}^{2}-qB_{0}\right)\leq 0.

Solutions exist when:

D:=\left(2KT_{YX}T_{XX}+qB_{1}\right)^{2}-4\left(KT_{XX}^{2}-qB_{2}\right)\left(KT_{YX}^{2}-qB_{0}\right)\geq 0.

The rest of the lemma is immediate from properties of solving quadratic inequalities.
∎

E.2.2
Proofs for Lemmas in Appendix D

Proof of
Lemma

7
.

The joint distribution of
\left(Y^{\prime},X^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}
is:

\left[\begin{array}[]{c}Y\\
X\end{array}\right]\sim N\left(\left[\begin{array}[]{c}Z\pi_{Y}\\
Z\pi\end{array}\right],\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}I_{n}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}&I_{n}\omega_{\zeta\eta}\\
I_{n}\omega_{\zeta\eta}&I_{n}\omega_{\eta\eta}\end{array}\right]\right).

Stack them together with their predicted values
PY=Z\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1}Z^{\prime}Y
and
PX=Z\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1}Z^{\prime}X
:

\left[\begin{array}[]{c}Y\\
X\\
P^{\prime}Y\\
P^{\prime}X\end{array}\right]\sim N\left(\left[\begin{array}[]{c}Z\pi_{Y}\\
Z\pi\\
Z\pi_{Y}\\
Z\pi\end{array}\right],\left[\begin{array}[]{cccc}I_{n}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}&I_{n}\omega_{\zeta\eta}&\omega_{\zeta\zeta}P^{\prime}&\omega_{\zeta\eta}P^{\prime}\\
I_{n}\omega_{\zeta\eta}&I_{n}\omega_{\eta\eta}&\omega_{\zeta\eta}P^{\prime}&\omega_{\eta\eta}P^{\prime}\\
\omega_{\zeta\zeta}P^{\prime}&\omega_{\zeta\eta}P^{\prime}&\omega_{\zeta\zeta}P^{\prime}&\omega_{\zeta\eta}P^{\prime}\\
\omega_{\zeta\eta}P^{\prime}&\omega_{\eta\eta}P^{\prime}&\omega_{\zeta\eta}P^{\prime}&\omega_{\eta\eta}P^{\prime}\end{array}\right]\right).

Then, the conditional normal distribution is:

\displaystyle\left[\begin{array}[]{c}Y\\
X\end{array}\right]|\left[\begin{array}[]{c}Z\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1}Z^{\prime}Y\\
Z\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1}Z^{\prime}X\end{array}\right]

\displaystyle\sim N\left(\left[\begin{array}[]{c}Z\pi_{Y}\\
Z\pi\end{array}\right]+\left[\begin{array}[]{c}Z\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1}Z^{\prime}Y-Z\pi_{Y}\\
Z\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1}Z^{\prime}X-Z\pi\end{array}\right],V\right)

\displaystyle=N\left(\left[\begin{array}[]{c}Z\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1}Z^{\prime}Y\\
Z\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1}Z^{\prime}X\end{array}\right],V\right)=N\left(\left[\begin{array}[]{c}PY\\
PX\end{array}\right],V\right)

Hence,
PX
and
PY
(i.e,
Z^{\prime}X
,
Z^{\prime}Y
) are sufficient
statistics for
\pi_{Y},\pi
.

To show that
\left(s_{1}^{\prime}s_{1},s_{1}^{\prime}s_{2},s_{2}^{\prime}s_{2}\right)
is a maximal invariant, let
F
be some conformable orthogonal matrix so
F^{\prime}F=I
.
For invariance, let
s_{1}^{*}=Fs_{1}
.
Then,
s_{1}^{*\prime}s_{1}^{*}=s_{1}^{\prime}F^{\prime}Fs_{1}=s_{1}^{\prime}s_{1}
.
Invariance of
\left(s_{1}^{\prime}s_{2},s_{2}^{\prime}s_{2}\right)
is analogous.
Maximality states that if
s_{1}^{*\prime}s_{1}^{*}=s_{1}^{\prime}s_{1}
, then
s_{1}^{*}=Fs_{1}
for some
F
.
Suppose not.
This means
s_{1}^{*}=Gs_{1}
, and
G
is not an orthogonal matrix but yet
s_{1}^{*\prime}s_{1}^{*}=s_{1}^{\prime}s_{1}
.
Since
G
is not an orthogonal matrix,
G^{\prime}G\neq I
.
Hence,
s_{1}^{*\prime}s_{1}^{*}=s_{1}^{\prime}G^{\prime}Gs_{1}\neq s_{1}^{\prime}s_{1}
,
a contradiction.
To obtain the distribution,

\displaystyle\left[\begin{array}[]{c}s_{1}\\
s_{2}\end{array}\right]

\displaystyle=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1/2}Z^{\prime}\left(Z\pi_{Y}+\zeta\right)\\
\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1/2}Z^{\prime}\left(Z\pi+\eta\right)\end{array}\right]=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{1/2}\pi_{Y}\\
\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{1/2}\pi\end{array}\right]+\left[\begin{array}[]{c}\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1/2}Z^{\prime}\zeta\\
\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1/2}Z^{\prime}\eta\end{array}\right].

Since
\textup{Var}\left(\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1/2}Z^{\prime}\eta\right)=\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1/2}Z^{\prime}\omega_{\eta\eta}Z\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{-1/2}=I_{K}\omega_{\eta\eta}
,

\left[\begin{array}[]{c}s_{1}\\
s_{2}\end{array}\right]\sim N\left(\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{1/2}\pi_{Y}\\
\left(Z^{\prime}Z\right)^{1/2}\pi\end{array}\right),\Omega\otimes I_{K}\right).

∎

Proof of
Lemma

8
.

I work out the
\mu
’s first.
Using the judge structure,
\sum_{i}M_{ii}^{2}=\sum_{k}\frac{\left(c-1\right)^{2}}{c},\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}=\sum_{k}\frac{c-1}{c}
.
We have also chosen
\pi_{k},\sigma_{\xi vk}
such that
\sum_{k}\pi_{k}=0,\sum_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}=0,\sum_{k}\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}=0
.
Then, we get the result for means:

\displaystyle\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mu_{1}\\
\mu_{2}\\
\mu_{3}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\sum_{k}\left(c-1\right)\left(\pi_{k}^{2}\beta^{2}+2\pi_{k}\beta\sigma_{\xi vk}+\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}\right)\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\sum_{k}\left(c-1\right)\left(\pi_{k}^{2}\beta+\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}\right)\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{K}}\sum_{k}\left(c-1\right)\pi_{k}^{2}\end{array}\right)=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\sqrt{K}\left(c-1\right)\left(s^{2}\beta^{2}+h^{2}\right)\\
\sqrt{K}\left(c-1\right)s^{2}\beta\\
\sqrt{K}\left(c-1\right)s^{2}\end{array}\right).

Using a derivation similar to that of the lemma for
V_{LM}
expression,

\displaystyle K\sigma_{22}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}\left(G_{ji}G_{ki}E\left[\zeta_{i}^{2}\right]R_{j}R_{k}+2G_{ij}G_{ki}E\left[\eta_{i}\zeta_{i}\right]R_{Yj}R_{k}+G_{ij}G_{ik}E\left[\eta_{i}^{2}\right]R_{Yj}R_{Yk}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\left(G_{ij}^{2}E\left[\eta_{i}^{2}\right]E\left[\zeta_{j}^{2}\right]+G_{ij}G_{ji}E\left[\eta_{i}\zeta_{i}\right]E\left[\eta_{j}\zeta_{j}\right]\right);

\displaystyle K\sigma_{11}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}E\left[\zeta_{i}^{2}\right]R_{Yj}R_{Yk}\left(G_{ji}G_{ki}+2G_{ij}G_{ki}+G_{ij}G_{ik}\right)+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}E\left[\zeta_{i}^{2}\right]E\left[\zeta_{j}^{2}\right]\left(G_{ij}^{2}+G_{ij}G_{ji}\right);

\displaystyle K\sigma_{33}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}E\left[\eta_{i}^{2}\right]R_{j}R_{k}\left(G_{ji}G_{ki}+2G_{ij}G_{ki}+G_{ij}G_{ik}\right)+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}E\left[\eta_{i}^{2}\right]E\left[\eta_{j}^{2}\right]\left(G_{ij}^{2}+G_{ij}G_{ji}\right);

\displaystyle K\sigma_{12}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}\left(G_{ji}G_{ki}E\left[\zeta_{i}^{2}\right]R_{j}R_{Yk}+2G_{ij}G_{ki}E\left[\zeta_{i}^{2}\right]R_{Yj}R_{k}+G_{ij}G_{ik}E\left[\eta_{i}\zeta_{i}\right]R_{Yj}R_{Yk}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}E\left[\eta_{i}\zeta_{i}\right]E\left[\zeta_{j}^{2}\right]\left(G_{ij}^{2}+G_{ij}G_{ji}\right);

\displaystyle K\sigma_{23}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}\left(G_{ji}G_{ki}E\left[\eta_{i}^{2}\right]R_{Yj}R_{k}+2G_{ij}G_{ki}E\left[\eta_{i}^{2}\right]R_{j}R_{Yk}+G_{ij}G_{ik}E\left[\eta_{i}\zeta_{i}\right]R_{j}R_{k}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}E\left[\eta_{i}\zeta_{i}\right]E\left[\eta_{j}^{2}\right]\left(G_{ij}^{2}+G_{ij}G_{ji}\right);\text{ and }

\displaystyle K\sigma_{13}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{k\neq i}E\left[\eta_{i}\zeta_{i}\right]R_{Yj}R_{k}\left(G_{ji}G_{ki}+2G_{ij}G_{ki}+G_{ij}G_{ik}\right)+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}E\left[\eta_{i}\zeta_{i}\right]E\left[\eta_{j}\zeta_{j}\right]\left(G_{ij}^{2}+G_{ij}G_{ji}\right).

The equalities hold regardless of whether identification is strong or weak and whether heterogeneity converges or not.
Without covariates,
G=P
is symmetric and the above expressions simplify. For instance,

\displaystyle K\sigma_{22}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\frac{\left(c-1\right)^{2}}{c}\left(\omega_{\zeta\zeta k}\pi_{k}^{2}+2\omega_{\zeta\eta k}\pi_{k}\pi_{Yk}+\omega_{\eta\eta k}\pi_{Yk}^{2}\right)+\sum_{k}\frac{c-1}{c}\left(\omega_{\eta\eta k}\omega_{\zeta\zeta k}+\omega_{\zeta\eta k}^{2}\right).

Evaluate the terms in the expression.
For higher moments of
\pi_{k}
,
\sum_{k}\pi_{k}^{2}=Ks^{2}
,
\sum_{k}\pi_{k}^{3}=0
, and
\sum_{k}\pi_{k}^{4}=Ks^{4}
.
Similarly,
\sum_{k}\pi_{k}^{3}\sigma_{\xi v}=0
.
Treating the heterogeneity in the same way,
\sum_{k}\sigma_{\xi v}^{2}=Kh^{2}
. Then,

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\omega_{\zeta\zeta k}\pi_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\left(\pi_{k}^{2}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\pi_{k}\beta\sigma_{\xi vk}+2\pi_{k}\sigma_{\varepsilon\xi}+\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}-\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)\pi_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle=s^{2}K\left(s^{2}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+h^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right);\text{ and }

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\omega_{\zeta\eta k}\pi_{k}\pi_{Yk}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\left(\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}+\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)\pi_{k}\left(\pi_{k}\beta+\sigma_{\xi vk}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}\pi_{k}^{2}+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\pi_{k}^{2}\beta+\pi_{k}^{2}\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}\right)=s^{2}K\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta+h^{2}\right).

Now, for the
P_{ij}^{2}
part,

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\omega_{\eta\eta k}\omega_{\zeta\zeta k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\sigma_{vv}\left(\pi_{k}^{2}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\pi_{k}\beta\sigma_{\xi vk}+2\pi_{k}\sigma_{\varepsilon\xi}+\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}-\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\sigma_{vv}\left(\pi_{k}^{2}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}-\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)

\displaystyle=K\sigma_{vv}\left(s^{2}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+h^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right);\text{ and }

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\omega_{\zeta\eta k}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\left(\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}+\sigma_{vv}\beta\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}+\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{vv}\beta\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\sigma_{vv}\beta\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\sum_{k}\left(\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}\sigma_{\varepsilon v}+\sigma_{vv}\beta\sigma_{\varepsilon v}+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\left(\pi_{k}^{2}\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+\sigma_{vv}^{2}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{vv}\beta\sigma_{\varepsilon v}+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}^{2}\right)=K\left(s^{2}h^{2}+\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)^{2}\right).

Combine the expressions for
\sigma_{22}
and impose asymptotics where
s\rightarrow 0
and
h\rightarrow 0
:

\displaystyle\sigma_{22}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\frac{\left(c-1\right)^{2}}{c}h^{2}+\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\frac{c-1}{c}\left(\sigma_{vv}\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+h^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)+\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)^{2}\right)+o(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{c-1}{c}\left(\sigma_{vv}\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)+\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)^{2}\right)+o(1).

Next, evaluate a few more sums that feature in the other
\sigma
expressions:

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}\pi_{Yk}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k}\left(\pi_{k}^{2}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\pi_{k}\beta\sigma_{\xi vk}+2\pi_{k}\sigma_{\varepsilon\xi}+\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\left(\pi_{k}^{2}\beta^{2}+2\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}+\sigma_{\xi v}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}\pi_{Yk}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\sigma_{\xi v}^{2}\left(\pi_{k}^{2}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\pi_{k}\beta\sigma_{\xi vk}+2\pi_{k}\sigma_{\varepsilon\xi}+\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)

\displaystyle=h^{2}\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+h^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)=o(1);

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\left(\pi_{k}^{2}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\pi_{k}\beta\sigma_{\xi vk}+2\pi_{k}\sigma_{\varepsilon\xi}+\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)^{2}=\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)^{2};

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\omega_{\zeta\eta}\pi_{Yk}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\left(\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}+\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)\left(\pi_{k}^{2}\beta^{2}+2\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}+\sigma_{\xi v}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=h^{2}\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)=o(1);\text{ and }

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\omega_{\zeta\eta}\omega_{\zeta\zeta}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\left(\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}+\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\left(\pi_{k}^{2}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\pi_{k}\beta\sigma_{\xi vk}+2\pi_{k}\sigma_{\varepsilon\xi}+\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k}\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)+o(1).

Using these results,

\displaystyle\sigma_{22}

\displaystyle=\frac{c-1}{c}\left(\sigma_{vv}\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)+\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)^{2}\right)+o(1);

\displaystyle\sigma_{11}

\displaystyle=2\frac{c-1}{c}\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)^{2}+o(1);

\displaystyle\sigma_{33}

\displaystyle=2\frac{c-1}{c}\sigma_{vv}^{2}+o(1);

\displaystyle\sigma_{12}

\displaystyle=2\frac{c-1}{c}\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)+o(1);

\displaystyle\sigma_{23}

\displaystyle=2\frac{c-1}{c}\sigma_{vv}\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)+o(1);\text{ and }

\displaystyle\sigma_{13}

\displaystyle=2\frac{c-1}{c}\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)^{2}+o(1).

Hence,
\sigma_{13}=\sigma_{23}^{2}/\sigma_{33}+o(1)
is immediate.
Further, for
\sigma_{12}
,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\sigma_{23}}{\sigma_{33}}

\displaystyle\left(\sigma_{22}-\frac{\sigma_{23}^{2}}{2\sigma_{33}}\right)=2\frac{\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}}{\sigma_{vv}}\left(\sigma_{22}-\frac{\left(2\frac{c-1}{c}\sigma_{vv}\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)\right)^{2}}{2\times 2\frac{c-1}{c}\sigma_{vv}^{2}}\right)+o(1)

\displaystyle=2\frac{c-1}{c}\left(\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\right)\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)+o(1)=\sigma_{12}+o(1).

Finally, the
\sigma_{11}
can be obtained:

\displaystyle\frac{4}{\sigma_{33}}\left(\sigma_{22}-\frac{\sigma_{23}^{2}}{2\sigma_{33}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\frac{c-1}{c}\sigma_{vv}^{2}}\left(\frac{c-1}{c}\left(\sigma_{vv}\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+h^{2}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)\right)\right)^{2}+o(1)

\displaystyle=2\frac{c-1}{c}\left(\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta\right)^{2}+o(1)=\sigma_{11}+o(1).

∎

E.2.3
Derivations for Simulations

Derivation for continuous setup without covariates
.

This subsection derives expressions for objects in the reduced-form model.
Comparing the first-stage equations,
\eta_{i}=v_{i}
.
As a corollary, for all
i
,
E\left[\eta_{i}^{2}\right]=\sigma_{vv}
.
Then,
\zeta_{i}=Z_{i}^{\prime}\left(\pi\beta_{i}-\pi_{Y}\right)+v_{i}\beta_{i}+\varepsilon_{i}
.
Define
\pi_{Y}
using
E\left[\zeta_{i}\right]=0
and
E\left[v_{i}\beta_{i}\right]=E\left[v_{i}\left(\beta+\xi_{i}\right)\right]=\sigma_{\xi vk(i)}
,
which implies
\pi_{Yk}=\pi_{k}\beta+\sigma_{\xi vk}
.
Hence, we can rewrite
\zeta_{i}
as:

\displaystyle\zeta_{i}

\displaystyle=\pi_{k(i)}\xi_{i}-\sigma_{\xi vk(i)}+v_{i}\beta+v_{i}\xi_{i}+\varepsilon_{i}.

By substituting the expression for
\zeta_{i}
, the covariance is
E\left[\eta_{i}\zeta_{i}\mid k\right]=\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}+\sigma_{vv}\beta+E\left[v_{i}^{2}\xi_{i}\right]+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}
.
By Isserlis’ theorem,
E\left[v_{i}^{2}\xi_{i}\right]=0
, so
E\left[\eta_{i}\zeta_{i}\mid k\right]=\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}+\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v}
.
The variance of
\zeta_{i}
can be derived analogously.
Since
E\left[v_{i}^{2}\beta_{i}^{2}\right]=\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}
by applying Isserlis’ theorem, with
\omega_{\eta\eta k}:=E[\eta_{i}^{2}\mid k(i)=k]
,
\omega_{\zeta\eta k}:=E[\zeta_{i}\eta_{i}\mid k(i)=k]
, and
\omega_{\zeta\zeta k}:=E[\zeta_{i}^{2}\mid k(i)=k]
, we obtain:

\begin{split}\omega_{\eta\eta k}&=\sigma_{vv}^{2},\\
\omega_{\zeta\eta k}&=\pi_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}+\sigma_{vv}\beta+\sigma_{\varepsilon v},\text{ and }\\
\omega_{\zeta\zeta k}&=\pi_{k}^{2}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\pi_{k}\beta\sigma_{\xi vk}+2\pi_{k}\sigma_{\varepsilon\xi}+\sigma_{\varepsilon\varepsilon}+\sigma_{\xi vk}^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\beta^{2}+\sigma_{vv}\sigma_{\xi\xi}+2\sigma_{\varepsilon v}\beta.\end{split}

(25)

In this model, the local average treatment effect (LATE) of judge
k
relative to the base judge
0
is:

LATE_{k}=\frac{\pi_{Yk}}{\pi_{k}}=\beta+\frac{\sigma_{\xi vk}}{\pi_{k}}.

(26)

Derivation for binary setup without covariates
.

The reduced-form residuals are given by:

\displaystyle\eta_{i}\mid v_{i}=\begin{cases}\begin{array}[]{c}1-\pi_{k}\\
-\pi_{k}\end{array}&\begin{array}[]{c}if\\
if\end{array}\begin{array}[]{c}v_{i}\leq\pi_{k}\\
v_{i}>\pi_{k}\end{array}\end{cases},\text{ and }\hskip 9.24994pt\zeta_{i}=\pi_{k(i)}\beta_{i}-\pi_{Yk(i)}+\eta_{i}\beta_{i}+\varepsilon_{i}.

Imposing
E\left[\zeta_{i}\right]=0
,
\pi_{Yk(i)}=\pi_{k(i)}\beta+E\left[\eta_{i}\beta_{i}\right]
, where
E\left[\eta_{i}\beta_{i}\right]=-\left(1-s\right)\left(2p-1\right)\sigma_{\xi vk}
.
Hence,

\pi_{Yk}=\pi_{k}\beta-\left(1-s\right)\left(2p-1\right)\sigma_{\xi vk}.

Due to the judge setup, the estimand is:

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{k}\pi_{Yk}\pi_{k}}{\sum_{k}\pi_{k}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{k}\left(\pi_{k}\beta-\left(1-s\right)\left(2p-1\right)\sigma_{\xi vk}\right)\pi_{k}}{\sum_{k}\pi_{k}^{2}}=\beta

because
\sum_{k}\sigma_{\xi vk}\pi_{k}=0
by construction.

Derivation for binary setup with covariates
.

Consider the structural model:

\displaystyle Y_{i}(x)

\displaystyle=x(\beta+\xi_{i})+w^{\prime}\gamma+\varepsilon_{i},\text{ and }

\displaystyle X_{i}(z)

\displaystyle=I\left\{z^{\prime}\pi+w^{\prime}\gamma-v_{i}\geq 0\right\}.

Let
\mathcal{N}_{t}
denote the set of observations in state
t
.
Then, using the
G
that corresponds to UJIVE,

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{t}}

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{t}\backslash{i}}G_{ij}R_{Yi}R_{j}=\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{t}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{t}\backslash{i}}G_{ij}\left(\pi_{Yk(i)}+\gamma_{t(i)}\right)\left(\pi_{k(j)}+\gamma_{t(j)}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{t}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{t}\backslash{i}}G_{ij}\left(\pi_{Yk(i)}\pi_{k(j)}+\gamma_{t(i)}\pi_{k(j)}+\pi_{Yk(i)}\gamma_{t(j)}+\gamma_{t(i)}\gamma_{t(j)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-1/5}\sum_{k\in\{0,t\}}5\times 4\times\frac{1}{5}\left(\pi_{Yk}\pi_{k}+\gamma_{t}\pi_{k}+\pi_{Yk}\gamma_{t}+\gamma_{t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{1}{1-1/10}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{t}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{t}\backslash{i}}\frac{1}{10}\left(\pi_{Yk(i)}\pi_{k(j)}+\gamma_{t}\pi_{k(j)}+\pi_{Yk(i)}\gamma_{t}+\gamma_{t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\in\{0,t\}}5\left(\pi_{Yk}\pi_{k}+\gamma_{t}\pi_{k}+\pi_{Yk}\gamma_{t}+\gamma_{t}^{2}\right)-\frac{1}{9}\sum_{k\in\{0,t\}}5\times 4\left(\pi_{Yk}\pi_{k}+\gamma_{Yt}\pi_{k}+\pi_{Yk}\gamma_{Xt}+\gamma_{t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{1}{9}5\times 5\left(\pi_{Yt}\pi_{0}+\gamma_{t}\pi_{0}+\pi_{Yt}\gamma_{t}+\gamma_{t}^{2}\right)-\frac{1}{9}5\times 5\left(\pi_{Y0}\pi_{t}+\gamma_{t}\pi_{t}+\pi_{Y0}\gamma_{t}+\gamma_{t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=5\left(\frac{5}{9}\right)\left(\pi_{Y0}\pi_{0}+\pi_{Yt}\pi_{t}-\pi_{Yt}\pi_{0}-\pi_{Y0}\pi_{t}\right).

Using the result that
\pi_{Yk}=\pi_{k}\beta-\left(1-s\right)\left(2p-1\right)\sigma_{\xi vk}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{t}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{t}\backslash{i}}G_{ij}R_{Yi}R_{j}

\displaystyle=5\left(\frac{5}{9}\right)\left(\pi_{Y0}\pi_{0}+\pi_{Yt}\pi_{t}-\pi_{Yt}\pi_{0}-\pi_{Y0}\pi_{t}\right)=\frac{25}{9}\pi_{Yt}\pi_{t}.

Analogously,
\sum_{i\in\mathcal{N}_{t}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{N}_{t}\backslash{i}}G_{ij}R_{i}R_{j}=\frac{25}{9}\pi_{t}^{2}
.
Hence, as long as
\sum_{t}\sigma_{\xi vt}\pi_{t}=0
, which is the case for the construction in the main text, we still recover
\beta
as our estimand:

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}R_{Yi}R_{j}}{\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}R_{i}R_{j}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{t}\pi_{Yt}\pi_{t}}{\sum_{t}\pi_{t}^{2}}=\frac{\sum_{t}\left(\pi_{t}\beta-\left(1-s\right)\left(2p-1\right)\sigma_{\xi vt}\right)\pi_{t}}{\sum_{t}\pi_{t}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\beta-\frac{\sum_{t}\left(1-s\right)\left(2p-1\right)\sigma_{\xi vt}\pi_{t}}{\sum_{t}\pi_{t}^{2}}=\beta,

regardless of
\gamma_{t}
.

E.2.4
Derivations for Variance Estimands

Proof of
Equation

19
.

\displaystyle E

\displaystyle\left[\hat{\Psi}_{MO}\right]=E\left[\sum_{i}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}\left(R_{j}+\eta_{j}\right)\right)^{2}\left(R_{\Delta i}+\nu_{i}\right)^{2}+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}^{2}\left(R_{i}+\eta_{i}\right)\left(R_{\Delta i}+\nu_{i}\right)\left(R_{j}+\eta_{j}\right)\left(R_{\Delta j}+\nu_{j}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=E\left[\sum_{i}\left(\left(\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}R_{j}\right)^{2}+\left(\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}\eta_{j}\right)^{2}\right)\left(R_{\Delta i}+\nu_{i}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+E\left[\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}^{2}\left(R_{i}R_{\Delta i}+\eta_{i}R_{\Delta i}+R_{i}\nu_{i}+\eta_{i}\nu_{i}\right)\left(R_{j}R_{\Delta j}+\eta_{j}R_{\Delta j}+R_{j}\nu_{j}+\eta_{j}\nu_{j}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}M_{ii}^{2}R_{i}^{2}\left(R_{\Delta i}^{2}+E\left[\nu_{i}^{2}\right]\right)+\sum_{i}R_{\Delta i}^{2}E\left[\left(\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}\eta_{j}\right)^{2}\right]+\sum_{i}E\left[\nu_{i}^{2}\right]E\left[\left(\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}\eta_{j}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}^{2}\left(R_{i}R_{\Delta i}+E\left[\eta_{i}\nu_{i}\right]\right)\left(R_{j}R_{\Delta j}+E\left[\eta_{j}\nu_{j}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}M_{ii}^{2}R_{i}^{2}\left(R_{\Delta i}^{2}+E\left[\nu_{i}^{2}\right]\right)+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}^{2}E\left[\eta_{j}^{2}\left(R_{\Delta i}^{2}+\nu_{i}^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}^{2}\left(R_{i}R_{\Delta i}+E\left[\eta_{i}\nu_{i}\right]\right)\left(R_{j}R_{\Delta j}+E\left[\eta_{j}\nu_{j}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}M_{ii}^{2}R_{i}^{2}R_{\Delta i}^{2}+\sum_{i}M_{ii}^{2}R_{i}^{2}E\left[\nu_{i}^{2}\right]+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}^{2}E\left[\nu_{i}^{2}\right]E\left[\eta_{j}^{2}\right]+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}^{2}R_{\Delta i}^{2}E\left[\eta_{j}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\sum_{i}\sum_{j\neq i}P_{ij}^{2}\left(R_{i}R_{\Delta i}R_{j}R_{\Delta j}+E\left[\eta_{i}\nu_{i}\right]R_{j}R_{\Delta j}+R_{i}R_{\Delta i}E\left[\eta_{j}\nu_{j}\right]+E\left[\eta_{i}\nu_{i}\right]E\left[\eta_{j}\nu_{j}\right]\right)

∎
